# Ruling log

Status: planning state M0. This document is the ruling log according to [ADR 0001](adr/0001-adrs-in-madr-format-decisions-as-ruling-log.md): decisions with
reason and location that do not have their own ADR or that an ADR only summarizes. Load-bearing
decisions are ADRs in the [ADR index](adr/README.md). Every chapter of the
specification lists its own decisions with numbers such as `R-AR-01` in the section
"Decisions of this chapter"; the source is there, the reference is here. Decisions that
concern only a particular installation (its accounts, names, hosts, repository paths) are
not here but in the private operations repository of that instance ([ADR 0002](adr/0002-two-repos-public-product-private-ops-repo.md)).

## Rules for this log

1. **IDs are stable.** An ID is never reassigned, never renumbered and never deleted. An
   outdated row stays in place and names "superseded by" or "Current state:" in the decision, with the
   new ID or location.
2. **Every row has a decision, a reason and a location.** The location is a link to file and section.
   `make linkcheck` checks files and anchors; a renamed section breaks the gate.
3. **The chapter wins.** If a row deviates from the chapter, the chapter applies (specification first,
   [PLAN 1](PLAN.md#1-principles) principle 3), and the row is adjusted in the same change.
4. **New entries.** Build tickets record their rulings in section 10. A change to the
   specification needs an ADR or an entry here.
5. **Owner questions** are listed with their default in `OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, product risks in
   `RISKS.md`; this log names only the decision that the plan uses until the answer.

## Reading guide: IDs

| Prefix | Meaning | Section |
|---|---|---|
| `D-B1` to `D-B13` | Fundamental decisions of the lead; in PLAN and chapters short `B1` to `B13` | 1 |
| `D-I1` to `D-I6` | Invariants of the backend policy and the terminal; short `I1` to `I6` | 2 |
| `D-R-<topic>` | Rulings of the lead from the research | 3 |
| `D-L1` to `D-L24`, `D-LS-<n>` | Rulings of the lead on deviations of the teams and further rulings without an ADR; short `L1` to `L24` | 4 |
| `D-<abbrev>-<n>` | Rulings of the planning teams, by chapter; the number counts the rulings of the chapter in the order of the team report and says nothing about the R-ID | 5 |
| `D-K-<Team><n>`, `D-K-X<n>`, `D-KB-<n>` | Core contract: requests from the teams, alignment of all documents, open items of the core report | 6 |
| `D-T1` to `D-T11` | Rulings of the lead on the addendum of 2026-10-10; short `T1` to `T11`. The later rulings of the lead `T12` (one session user per person), `T13` (no shared locations) and `T14` (preview images `terminal-*` and `viz-*`) are D-W-57, D-W-62 and D-W-77 in section 9; they are not `D-T12` to `D-T14` | 7, 9 |
| `D-T12` onward, `D-V<n>`, `D-N<n>` | Rulings of the writers of the addendum; the column "Writer ID" names their original number | 7 |
| `D-G-<n>` | Rulings of the gap integration that are in no chapter; `<n>` is the number of the gap item | 8 |
| `D-W-<n>` | Ruling log of the resumption | 9 |
| `R-<abbrev>-<n>` | Decision of a chapter; the abbreviation names the chapter: `PR` 01, `AR` 02, `DM` 03, `SY` 04, `AZ` 05, `API` 06, `RE` 07, `ID` 08, `AG` 09, `EX` 11, `FI` 12, `PN` 13, `SU` 14, `CL` 15, `OP` 16, `OB` 17, `RL` 18, `SE` 19, `DP` 20, `DV` 21, `IM` 22, `QA` 23, `TM` 24, `VZ` 25 | 8 |
| `P-<n>`, `W<n>` | Decisions and known contradictions of the plan ([PLAN 19](PLAN.md#19-decisions-of-this-plan), [PLAN 7](PLAN.md#7-m1-foundation-toolchain-and-server-core-with-permissions)) | 8 |

Chapter references name the number of the chapter and of the section, for example "02 3.3" for
[02 3.3](spec/02-architecture.md#33-commands-of-the-binary). The link leads to the section.

## 1. Fundamental decisions of the lead

Fixed architecture decisions that all teams used in the same way.

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-B1 | Modular monolith in Go: one binary with strict, hexagonal modules and the roles `api`, `worker`, `all`; no microservices. The binary is called `bothy` (D-L1). | A single host is the first target: one deployment, no distributed transactions, low latency, simple backup. The roles allow splitting off later without restructuring. | [ADR 0004](adr/0004-modular-monolith-instead-of-microservices.md), [ADR 0008](adr/0008-hexagonal-modules-and-checked-dependency-rules.md), [02 3.2](spec/02-architecture.md#32-roles-of-a-process) |
| D-B2 | Server in Go. OpenAPI 3.1 and AsyncAPI 3.0 are normative (spec-first); server stubs and clients are generated. SQL is typed (sqlc), migrations are versioned. | Static binary, small images for several architectures, concurrency for WebSockets, mature OTel support. Python remains for the SDK and the Hermes adapter. | [ADR 0005](adr/0005-go-as-the-server-language.md), [ADR 0013](adr/0013-spec-first-openapi-31-and-asyncapi-30-normative.md), [ADR 0007](adr/0007-typed-database-access-sqlc-and-migration-tool.md) |
| D-B3 | PostgreSQL from day 1, no SQLite in the server; access only through the ports of the modules; address and port of the database are configuration. | Full-text search, concurrency, managed Postgres services at all cloud providers, one database path instead of two. | [ADR 0006](adr/0006-postgresql-from-day-1-instead-of-sqlite.md), [03 1](spec/03-data-model.md#1-principles) |
| D-B4 | Files behind an object storage interface (S3 API) with the drivers file system (default) and S3-compatible. | The same code runs on a single host and in the cloud. | [ADR 0010](adr/0010-object-storage-behind-an-s3-interface-local-driver-as-default.md), [12 4](spec/12-files.md#4-object-storage) |
| D-B5 | Apps with Flutter from one codebase for iOS, Android, macOS and Windows. The web target is only the admin and statistics console (with terminal since the addendum, D-T4), no chat in the browser. | All four target platforms with one codebase; design system and API client are shared. | [ADR 0027](adr/0027-flutter-for-all-apps.md), [ADR 0028](adr/0028-web-target-only-for-the-admin-and-statistics-console.md), [ADR 0070](adr/0070-terminal-in-the-web-console.md) |
| D-B6 | Plugins and bots never run in the server process. Extension only through the public API: principals with token and grants, gateway WebSocket, signed webhooks, app manifest. | Isolation and security; the product uses the public API itself from the start, Hermes Agent is only one client among several. | [ADR 0022](adr/0022-agents-and-plugins-outside-the-server-process.md), [11 1](spec/11-extensions.md#1-principles) |
| D-B7 | One function `authorize(principal, action, resource)` in the module `authz` checks every route, every event, every delivery, every push, every search, every file fetch and every agent invocation. Without view permission the server answers `404`, not `403`. A test fails on every route and every event without an action. | One single decision point instead of scattered permission logic; no information about the existence of other people's objects. | [ADR 0017](adr/0017-one-authorization-function-404-instead-of-403-mandatory-registration.md), [05 1](spec/05-actions.md#1-binding-rules), [07 4](spec/07-permissions.md#4-the-authorize-function) |
| D-B8 | Event log with a global, commit-ordered sequence (counter row with a row lock in the same transaction); catch-up "everything from number X", filtered by `authorize`; in addition a sequence per thread. Every write request carries a client ID (UUIDv7); a repetition returns the original. | `bigserial` is not commit-ordered; only this way does a catching-up client lose nothing. | [ADR 0009](adr/0009-commit-ordered-event-log-with-a-global-sequence.md), [04 3](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#3-global-commit-ordered-sequence), [04 5](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#5-idempotent-writes) |
| D-B9 | Identity through a provider interface: OIDC, header of a trusted reverse proxy (only with loopback), local accounts. Device binding with DPoP (RFC 9449); admin actions require a fresh step-up. | No requirement for a particular network tool in the product; a stolen token is useless without the device. | [ADR 0019](adr/0019-identity-providers-oidc-proxy-header-local-passkey-accounts.md), [ADR 0020](adr/0020-device-binding-dpop-step-up-for-admin-actions.md), [08 2](spec/08-identity.md#2-identity-providers) |
| D-B10 | Agent runtimes run outside the server. The server knows agents as principals, runs as objects and one backend policy per agent; it never stores Claude.ai credentials or subscription tokens. | Anthropic's terms forbid collecting, storing or brokering such credentials (quotes in [10 2](spec/10-backend-policy.md#2-rationale-from-the-anthropic-terms)). | [ADR 0022](adr/0022-agents-and-plugins-outside-the-server-process.md), [ADR 0024](adr/0024-backend-policy-personal-organization-with-invariant.md), [ADR 0025](adr/0025-run-as-its-own-object-for-the-live-box.md) |
| D-B11 | Telemetry with OpenTelemetry, export only through an OTel Collector. The audit log is separate from it (own table, hash-chained, own retention). No message contents in telemetry. | Vendor-neutral; audit needs different guarantees than operational data; privacy. | [ADR 0034](adr/0034-opentelemetry-through-a-collector-local-default-stack.md), [ADR 0021](adr/0021-hash-chained-audit-log-separate-from-telemetry.md), [17 2](spec/17-observability.md#2-basic-rules) |
| D-B12 | Distribution as OCI images for `linux/amd64` and `linux/arm64`, Docker Compose as a one-command installation, OpenTofu modules per target. Configuration according to 12-factor, secrets through a provider interface. The server does not depend on macOS. | The server should run the same way on a single host and in any cloud. | [ADR 0012](adr/0012-oci-images-compose-opentofu.md), [ADR 0011](adr/0011-12-factor-configuration-and-secret-provider.md), [16 3](spec/16-operations.md#3-deployment-targets) |
| D-B13 | Single tenant: one instance is one organization and one "server" in the Discord sense; no server bar, no invitation links for outsiders. Several tenants mean several instances. | Smaller attack surface and simpler permissions; separation through installations instead of through code. | [00 Instance](spec/00-glossary.md#00-glossary), [01 3](spec/01-product.md#3-feature-list) |

## 2. Invariants

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-I1 | An agent with `billing_mode = personal` may only be triggered by its `credential_holder`. The server checks this transactionally on every change to grants, groups, owner list, operator and policy and rejects every change that would violate it. No owner exception (D-L22). | A personal subscription serves only its person's own use. | [10 3](spec/10-backend-policy.md#3-invariants), [10 5](spec/10-backend-policy.md#5-i1-transactional), [03 6.4](spec/03-data-model.md#64-i1-check), [ADR 0024](adr/0024-backend-policy-personal-organization-with-invariant.md) |
| D-I2 | The dispatch path checks again immediately before every agent invocation; if the triggering person does not match, there is no invocation (fail closed, audit). | A second safety net next to the transactional check. | [10 6](spec/10-backend-policy.md#6-dispatch-check-i2) |
| D-I3 | The server signs every invocation with a short-lived invocation token. The adapter checks the signature and the configured mode against its proven reported mode and starts no run on a mismatch (`billing_mode_mismatch`). | The server does not see the runtime; only the adapter can prove the reported mode. | [10 7](spec/10-backend-policy.md#7-adapter-check-i3), [ADR 0023](adr/0023-signed-invocation-token-and-check-in-the-adapter.md) |
| D-I4 | Switching the policy is an admin action with step-up, reason and audit, global or per agent; to `personal` only if I1 holds afterwards. | Cost bearer and terms change only deliberately. | [10 9](spec/10-backend-policy.md#9-switching-i4) |
| D-I5 | The server knows no credentials. The API key is with the adapter in its secret provider; the personal subscription stays in the runtime of the person. | D-B10. | [10 3](spec/10-backend-policy.md#3-invariants) |
| D-I6 | If `llm_credentials = personal` is declared on a host, only `credential_holder_id` may open a terminal there, not even another owner. Checked transactionally on grants and on every open. | A terminal allows any use of the login of the OS user; a personal subscription must not run for others (D-T2). | [24 7.3](spec/24-terminal.md#73-invariant-i6), [03 6.8](spec/03-data-model.md#68-i6-check), [ADR 0068](adr/0068-terminal-through-a-host-service-with-noise-stream-and-invariant-i6.md) |

## 3. Rulings from the research

The lead decided the research before the teams. Evidence with URL and retrieval date is in the
named ADRs and chapters; versions are pinned in [toolchain.md](quality/toolchain.md).

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-R-STACK | Go, PostgreSQL, pgx with sqlc, migrations with goose, oapi-codegen (strict server, conservative spec without multi-type unions), coder/websocket, River for background jobs without Pro features, go-webauthn, OTel with otelhttp and otelpgx. `LISTEN/NOTIFY` only as a wake-up signal without content, fan-out in the process. | Mature libraries with free licenses; goose works with plain SQL; a `NOTIFY` with content costs a lock at commit. | [ADR 0005](adr/0005-go-as-the-server-language.md), [ADR 0007](adr/0007-typed-database-access-sqlc-and-migration-tool.md), [R-AR-09](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SY-15](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-R-OBJEKTSPEICHER | File system driver as the default; the S3 driver uses only core S3 (PUT, GET, DELETE, multipart, presigned PUT). For self-hosted S3 the product documents SeaweedFS, not MinIO. Images in pure Go. Current state: metadata is removed losslessly according to D-L16. | Broad compatibility without special features; reasons and evidence on MinIO in [ADR 0010](adr/0010-object-storage-behind-an-s3-interface-local-driver-as-default.md). | [ADR 0010](adr/0010-object-storage-behind-an-s3-interface-local-driver-as-default.md), [ADR 0047](adr/0047-remove-image-metadata-losslessly.md), [R-FI-07](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-R-CLIENTS | Clients for TypeScript (openapi-typescript), Python (openapi-python-client) and Dart (openapi-generator with dart-dio) are generated from the spec and compiled in the gate. | Generated clients cannot deviate from the contract. | [ADR 0026](adr/0026-sdks-generated-core-plus-hand-written-layer.md), [06 14](spec/06-api.md#14-code-generation) |
| D-R-DOKU | MkDocs with Material, pinned; switch to Zensical only when a stable version and the features in use are proven. Diagrams with mermaid-cli. | Material is in maintenance mode, Zensical is not yet stable. | [ADR 0045](adr/0045-documentation-site-generator.md) |
| D-R-PASSKEYS | Device binding and step-up through a device key in secure storage with user confirmation; WebAuthn passkeys for the web console and for instances with a public domain; local accounts additionally with password and TOTP as a fallback that can be switched off. | Native Apple apps need a publicly reachable Associated Domain for passkeys; private instances would otherwise have no step-up. | [ADR 0019](adr/0019-identity-providers-oidc-proxy-header-local-passkey-accounts.md), [ADR 0020](adr/0020-device-binding-dpop-step-up-for-admin-actions.md), [08 6](spec/08-identity.md#6-device-keys-and-step-up) |
| D-R-PUSH | iOS and macOS: visible push with `mutable-content`, only IDs and a neutral placeholder; the Notification Service Extension fetches the content through the API. Android: FCM data message with IDs only. Windows: the app in the notification area holds the connection and shows local notifications. Push keys are instance configuration. | No message content at Apple or Google. | [ADR 0031](adr/0031-push-without-content-the-app-fetches-the-content.md), [13 5](spec/13-push-notifications.md#5-delivery-paths-per-platform) |
| D-R-VERTEILUNG | macOS with Developer ID, notarization and Sparkle 2; iOS through TestFlight; Windows as MSIX with `.appinstaller`; Android through a test channel of the publisher's choice. Current state: macOS only as a Developer ID edition ([R-RL-06](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter)). | Updates and package identity without a store requirement on the desktop. | [ADR 0037](adr/0037-app-distribution-and-auto-update-channels.md), [18 8](spec/18-release.md#8-app-distribution) |
| D-R-HERMES | Reference adapter for Hermes Agent as a platform plugin; the progress is mapped to the run; the adapter recognizes the reported mode only by the type of the key, never by its value. | Verified public extension points instead of text parsing (D-L7). | [09 17](spec/09-agents.md#17-reference-adapter-hermes-agent) |
| D-R-RUNNER | The Claude Code runner starts `claude -p` with stream-json, one session per thread, the environment as an allow-list, follow-ups over stdin and stop by signal. Costs only as an estimate. Current state: the pre-check of the reported mode is binding ([R-AG-20](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter)). | Enforce conditions through the environment instead of assuming them. | [09 18](spec/09-agents.md#18-reference-adapter-claude-code-runner-development-forums) |
| D-R-ANTHROPIC | The personal mode is intended only for runtimes in which a person uses their own subscription for their own use. The product does not collect, store or broker Claude.ai credentials. Whether a particular runtime is permitted is the responsibility of the instance operator. | https://code.claude.com/docs/en/legal-and-compliance, retrieved 2026-10-10 [verified]; quotes and conclusions in 10. | [10 2](spec/10-backend-policy.md#2-rationale-from-the-anthropic-terms) |
| D-R-TELEMETRIE | Collector with exporters per backend; `gen_ai.*` attributes encapsulated in one place; telemetry never blocks the message path (queue, batch, fail-open, counter for dropped data); dashboards as files; SLOs with Sloth and burn-rate alerts. | Vendor-neutral; unstable conventions stay replaceable in one place. | [ADR 0034](adr/0034-opentelemetry-through-a-collector-local-default-stack.md), [ADR 0035](adr/0035-slos-with-error-budgets-and-burn-rate-alerts.md), [signals.md 6](observability/signals.md#6-genai-conventions-encapsulated) |
| D-R-QUALITAET | Dart locally with `flutter analyze` and `custom_lint`, publicly also SonarQube Cloud; one Sonar profile in the repository; actions pinned by SHA, zizmor and actionlint mandatory, no `pull_request_target`; Trivy only pinned, OSV-Scanner for dependencies; gitleaks via CLI; license gate. | The local SonarQube Community Build does not analyze Dart; protection of the supply chain. | [ADR 0038](adr/0038-local-gates-first-one-command-identical-to-ci.md), [ADR 0039](adr/0039-only-github-hosted-runners-public-from-the-first-push.md), [ADR 0040](adr/0040-sonarqube-local-and-cloud-with-the-same-profile-and-gate.md), [23 7](spec/23-quality.md#7-sonarqube) |

## 4. Rulings of the lead

### 4.1 Deviations of the teams (L1 to L24)

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-L1 | One binary `bothy` with subcommands (`serve`, `migrate`, `setup`, `config`, `import`; W3 adds more); role via `--role` with `api`, `worker` or `all`. "server" in B1 means the server role. | One image, one name. | [R-AR-01](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [02 3.3](spec/02-architecture.md#33-commands-of-the-binary), [PLAN W3](PLAN.md#known-contradictions-that-m1-resolves) |
| D-L2 | Health (`live`, `ready`) is on an internal listener outside the public API and is not in `openapi.yaml`; the public status page remains an API resource. | Health is an operations contract; the public listener gets no path without login. | [16 4.2](spec/16-operations.md#42-two-listeners), [R-API-16](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AZ-22](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter), [PLAN W2](PLAN.md#known-contradictions-that-m1-resolves) |
| D-L3 | `gateway`, `search` and `stats` are separate modules. Current state: since the addendum, `stats` owns the table `stats_minutes` (D-V03). | Own ports, dependencies and performance targets. | [R-AR-02](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [02 4.2](spec/02-architecture.md#42-module-list) |
| D-L4 | The source of the configuration values are the tables "Configuration values of this chapter"; the configuration reference is generated from them and checked in the gate. From M1 on, the schema in the code is the source. No name twice. | One source per value. | [configuration.md 1](operations/configuration.md#1-sources), [PLAN M1-08](PLAN.md#known-contradictions-that-m1-resolves) |
| D-L5 | All repository content is English (D-W-94, owner, 2026-10-11). Before: descriptions in OpenAPI and AsyncAPI and runtime texts of the operations artifacts are English; planning documents German. | Descriptions become comments in generated code, runtime texts are read by instance operators; since D-W-94 the same holds for every document. | [R-API-15](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OB-09](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-L6 | Token prefix `bothy_`; [ADR 0044](adr/0044-working-name-and-branding-as-configuration.md) lists it as a place with the working name. | Secret scanners need a unique prefix. | [ADR 0061](adr/0061-token-format-with-a-fixed-prefix-and-checksum.md), [ADR 0044](adr/0044-working-name-and-branding-as-configuration.md) |
| D-L7 | Hermes adapter without a progress bubble: `format_tool_event` creates run steps; `send_draft` and the status fill the live box. | Verified public override points instead of text parsing. | [R-AG-18](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [09 17.3](spec/09-agents.md#173-mapping-of-the-adapter-methods) |
| D-L8 | For runtimes with both modes, `reported_billing_mode` is missing; instead the adapter reports `billing_modes_supported`. The check per run is binding. | The runner chooses the mode per run. | [09 4.8](spec/09-agents.md#48-heartbeat-and-reported-state), [10 8](spec/10-backend-policy.md#8-expected-and-reported) |
| D-L9 | The typing indicator follows the run state; agents send no typing. | Normative in the core contract. | [04 12](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#12-typing-and-agent-presence) |
| D-L10 | Two device keys: a DPoP key without confirmation per signature, a step-up key with confirmation. | The Notification Service Extension must sign without a user interface. | [R-ID-04](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-CL-05](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-L11 | Font Atkinson Hyperlegible, system font as a fallback. | Unambiguous characters, easy to read, license OFL. | [typography.md](design/typography.md) |
| D-L12 | Minimum versions Android API 26 and Windows 10 2004. | Crash reports and Share Target require them. | [R-CL-12](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [15 2](spec/15-client-architecture.md#2-platforms) |
| D-L13 | Additional packages and files of teams E, F and H are adopted. | Justified in the team rulings, section 5. | [R-CL-01](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [design/README.md](design/README.md#files), [23 3](spec/23-quality.md#3-component-contract) |
| D-L14 | Own Postgres image with pgBackRest. | WAL archiving needs the tool in the database container. | [R-OP-07](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-L15 | Additional burn-rate level 3 as a ticket alert. | Default of Sloth. | [ADR 0035](adr/0035-slos-with-error-budgets-and-burn-rate-alerts.md), [slos.md 3](observability/slos.md#3-burn-rate-alerts) |
| D-L16 | Image metadata of JPEG and PNG is removed losslessly from the original on upload (filter on segments and chunks); thumbnails are re-encoded. | Privacy without loss of quality of the original. | [ADR 0047](adr/0047-remove-image-metadata-losslessly.md), [R-FI-07](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SE-06](spec/19-security.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-L17 | The portability test runs on fresh GitHub-hosted Linux runners. Current state: all jobs run on GitHub-hosted runners (D-W-92). | A fresh Linux runner is the purpose of the portability test. | [ADR 0039](adr/0039-only-github-hosted-runners-public-from-the-first-push.md) |
| D-L18 | The deny list rightly reports identities of the organization in commits. Before publication the public identity is fixed; older commits are rewritten or the history starts anew. Partly superseded by D-W-06: the owner has chosen the identity, rewriting the authors is no longer needed. | White label of the product. | [public-switch.md A](quality/public-switch.md#a-before-the-push) |
| D-L19 | The target repository of the owner is a matter of the instance and is only in the operations repository; the product holds the placeholder `OWNER`. The recommendation of a neutral organization instead of a personal account is an owner question. | Otherwise the account name would be in the Go module path. | [02 6](spec/02-architecture.md#6-code-layout), [public-switch.md A](quality/public-switch.md#a-before-the-push) |
| D-L20 | Dev isolation: one OS user alone is not enough; a VM or equivalent. | A shared kernel and misconfiguration open the host. | [ADR 0046](adr/0046-multi-user-dev-environment-only-with-isolation.md), [R-DV-01](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-L21 | Direct messages are invisible to owners; access only through the privacy or legal route with step-up and audit. Owner question with this default. | Confidentiality between people. | [R-AZ-02](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter), [07 SR6](spec/07-permissions.md#sr6-direct-messages-only-for-participants) |
| D-L22 | I1 without an owner exception: `personal` works only as long as the `credential_holder` is the only owner. | Owners have `trigger` everywhere. | [ADR 0024](adr/0024-backend-policy-personal-organization-with-invariant.md), [10 3](spec/10-backend-policy.md#3-invariants) |
| D-L23 | A revocation aborts running runs of the person concerned (fail closed), deviating from study C. | Security before convenience. | [R-SY-11](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [04 8.3](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#83-what-the-client-receives-and-does-on-access-removal) |
| D-L24 | Invocation token with EdDSA and JWKS instead of HMAC. | The adapter cannot forge a token; keys can be rotated. | [R-SY-08](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0023](adr/0023-signed-invocation-token-and-check-in-the-adapter.md), [ADR 0067](adr/0067-jose-profile-with-the-standard-library.md) |

From the proposals of the teams, the lead determined ADRs 0047 to 0065; they are in the
[ADR index](adr/README.md).

### 4.2 Further rulings of the lead without their own ADR

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-LS-01 | Automatic adoption of disablements from the identity provider (SCIM, Shared Signals) comes later with its own ADR. | Until then the owner disables a person immediately with `user.disable`; a session lives at most until `auth.idp_recheck_interval`. | [08 3.5](spec/08-identity.md#35-delay-when-disabled-at-the-provider), [ADR 0019](adr/0019-identity-providers-oidc-proxy-header-local-passkey-accounts.md) |
| D-LS-02 | Platform attestation of the device keys later; today it is stored but not required (`auth.require_key_attestation`). | Without attestation, hardware binding cannot be proven; it becomes mandatory only once the platforms supply it. | [08 auth.require_key_attestation](spec/08-identity.md#61-two-keys-per-native-device), [ADR 0020](adr/0020-device-binding-dpop-step-up-for-admin-actions.md) |
| D-LS-03 | An identity driver through the local interface of the network agent (`WhoIs`) is planned as a later driver, not in M1. | A path without trust in headers; for the start the proxy header with loopback is enough. | [08 2.4](spec/08-identity.md#24-trusted-proxy-header) |
| D-LS-04 | Checkpoints of the audit log are kept outside the database (`audit.checkpoint_sink`). Immutable storage (WORM) is a matter of operations; the operations documentation describes it: an S3 bucket with Object Lock in compliance mode in a separate account, retention at least `retention.audit`, until M7-22 a copy from the file system target by the owner ([backup-restore.md 8](operations/backup-restore.md#8-immutable-storage-of-the-audit-checkpoints)). | The hash chain alone does not detect a complete rewrite by the database administration. | [19 SEC-AUD-2](spec/19-security.md#12-supply-chain-backups-and-audit-integrity), [ADR 0021](adr/0021-hash-chained-audit-log-separate-from-telemetry.md) |
| D-LS-05 | Webhook as a third transport for agents. | Adapters without a permanently running process. | [09 4.2](spec/09-agents.md#42-transports), [04 15.6](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#156-webhook-as-transport) |
| D-LS-06 | Built-in incoming webhook formats only `native` and `alertmanager`. | Little server code for foreign formats; everything else becomes an app. | [R-EX-08](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-LS-07 | Reserved control commands. | Unambiguous, translatable, never sent to the model. | [R-AG-13](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [09 10.1](spec/09-agents.md#101-built-in-commands) |
| D-LS-08 | An owner exception in I1 is rejected. | Like D-L22. | [ADR 0024](adr/0024-backend-policy-personal-organization-with-invariant.md) |
| D-LS-09 | Icon set Phosphor. Location deviating from the list of the lead: the design documentation, not [ADR 0030](adr/0030-design-tokens-as-one-source-with-a-generator.md). | Filled variant for states, distinctive look. | [icons.md](design/icons.md) |
| D-LS-10 | Font according to D-L11. Location as for D-LS-09. | Like D-L11. | [typography.md](design/typography.md) |
| D-LS-11 | The switch to Zensical follows the criteria in [ADR 0045](adr/0045-documentation-site-generator.md). | Like D-R-DOKU. | [ADR 0045](adr/0045-documentation-site-generator.md) |
| D-LS-12 | Merge into `main` only by squash. | GitHub does not sign the new commits of rebase merges; signed commits and a linear history go together only with squash. | [push-discipline.md](quality/push-discipline.md#protection-of-main-ruleset) |
| D-LS-13 | A separate preview token per push registration; 08 is aligned with it. | Two processes on the same refresh family trigger the theft detection. | [R-PN-07](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [08 preview token](spec/08-identity.md#74-refresh-tokens-rotation-families-reuse) |
| D-LS-14 | An agent invocation with attachments is written only when all attachments are final. | Agents never see unchecked bytes. | [R-SY-20](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-FI-09](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-LS-15 | Search as a coordinator with read ports of the owning modules. | Tables of other modules only through ports. | [R-SU-09](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [02 4.3](spec/02-architecture.md#43-ports-events-and-sources-per-module) |
| D-LS-16 | A backend policy per dev environment comes later; until then only `organization`. | I1 is defined per agent. | [R-DV-05](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DM-24](spec/03-data-model.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-LS-17 | Read-only channels (`channels.read_only`) as an addition to the core contract. | Only a state on the channel can also keep owners from writing (read mirror of the import). | [R-DM-25](spec/03-data-model.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AZ-12](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-LS-18 | Command-line tools log in as a device with `platform = 'cli'`; the general rules of 08 apply. Done: 08 section 6.4 specifies that a `cli` device has no step-up key and performs no step-up; actions with step-up go through the app or the web console (R-ID-17, ID-T29). | A key in a tool signs without user confirmation; without attestation the server cannot tell it apart from a key in secure storage. No step-up is the safer choice, as with API tokens. | [03 `devices`](spec/03-data-model.md#devices), [08 6.4](spec/08-identity.md#64-command-line-tools) |
| D-LS-19 | Import through a source-neutral import interface of the server. | The import tool runs outside the server ([R-IM-01](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter)). | [22 8](spec/22-import-migration.md#8-import-interface-of-the-server), [06 applyImportBatch](spec/06-api.md#7-idempotency) |

## 5. Rulings of the teams

Every planning team reported its decisions beyond the basis. Most of them are R-IDs in the chapter
today; the column "Location" leads there. Where the chapter changed the decision later, the row says
so with "Current state:". Instance-specific rulings of the migration team are in the operations repository
(D-IM-09).

### 01 Product

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-PR-01 | Four columns with a session column instead of a member list; no presence and no read receipts for people; no keyboard shortcut triggers an approval; questions from one's own runs notify even with "mute"; quiet hours make notifications silent; on mobile only emergency administration; accent only from the palette; link preview in the client later and only with opt-in. | Reasons per ruling in the chapter. | [R-PR-01](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-02](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-03](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-04](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-05](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-06](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-07](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-08](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PR-09](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 02 Architecture

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-AR-01 | One binary `bothy` with subcommands, roles via `--role`, default `all`. | One image for all roles; splitting them costs one line of configuration. | [R-AR-01](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-02 | Modules `gateway`, `search`, `stats` in addition to the modules from 03. Current state: `stats` owns `stats_minutes` (D-V03). | Own ports and performance targets. | [R-AR-02](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-03 | The port of a module is the root package `internal/<module>` with its own DTOs. | The domain stays changeable internally; a later process boundary needs no rework of the callers. | [R-AR-03](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-04 | Module dependencies form a graph without cycles; the reverse direction only through source ports or in-tx events. | Separability and no hidden cycles. | [R-AR-04](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-05 | Cross-cutting ports in `internal/kernel`. | As edges between modules they would create cycles. | [R-AR-05](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-06 | Unit of Work with a fixed order of phases before the commit. | Enforces the lock order from 03 in one central place. | [R-AR-06](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-07 | No external calls inside transactions. | Short serialized commit phase, safe retry. | [R-AR-07](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-08 | Push and outgoing webhooks are produced in the worker from the event log through consumers with a cursor. | Producers know no consumers; the log already is the outbox. | [R-AR-08](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0051](adr/0051-worker-consumers-of-the-event-log-with-a-cursor.md) |
| D-AR-09 | River only in `platform/jobs`, own schema, no Pro features, job arguments only IDs. | Dependency in version 0.x in one place; no further storage location for content. | [R-AR-09](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-10 | Ephemeral events between nodes only in mode `multi`, via `NOTIFY` with IDs. | `NOTIFY` stays a wake-up signal without content. | [R-AR-10](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-11 | Gateway sessions only in the process; `resume` on another node ends with `4006`. | No shared session store; `identify` with a cursor catches up just as well. | [R-AR-11](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-12 | Rate limits in memory per process. | No additional service; limits are protection, not billing. | [R-AR-12](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-13 | Health on the internal listener outside the public API. Current state: metrics only via OTLP, no metrics endpoint ([ADR 0034](adr/0034-opentelemetry-through-a-collector-local-default-stack.md)). | For the orchestrator without login, but not public. | [R-AR-13](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), D-L2 |
| D-AR-14 | Proof per request that `authorize` ran with the action of the route. | Holds D-B7 at runtime. | [R-AR-14](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-15 | Configuration (instance operator, restart) separate from settings (owner, database, immediate); unknown keys abort the start. | Clear responsibility; typos show up at start. | [R-AR-15](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0011](adr/0011-12-factor-configuration-and-secret-provider.md) |
| D-AR-16 | Migrations only via `bothy migrate`, never at start; the server checks the schema version. | No simultaneous migration by several nodes, no restart loop. | [R-AR-16](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-17 | Fixed order at shutdown; WebSockets close with `4013`. | `http.Server.Shutdown` does not cover hijacked connections. | [R-AR-17](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-18 | In the request path, only a retry of the whole Unit of Work on deadlock and lock timeout. | Other retries belong in jobs or clients. | [R-AR-18](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AR-19 | Architecture gate with go-arch-lint, configuration generated from the module register. | One source for module boundaries. | [R-AR-19](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0050](adr/0050-module-register-as-the-source-for-architecture-gate-and-table-ownership.md) |
| D-AR-20 | Performance targets as named values, one load test or benchmark per target. | No number without a test. | [R-AR-20](spec/02-architecture.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0063](adr/0063-performance-gate-for-the-event-log.md) |
| D-AR-21 | C4 diagrams as Mermaid flowcharts, containers and components with ELK layout. | The C4 mode of Mermaid overlapped labels; ELK renders cleanly. | [architecture/README.md](architecture/README.md#conventions) |
| D-AR-22 | ERDs by module group; every table exactly once; complete columns only in 03. | Readability; no second truth. | [architecture/README.md](architecture/README.md#conventions) |

### 06 API

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-API-01 | Path prefix `/api/v1` for all surfaces, including gateway and incoming webhooks; server URL in `openapi.yaml` relative. | One prefix; every self-hosted instance has its own host. | [R-API-01](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-02 | No JSON `null` on the wire; removal through dedicated fields or modes. | The Dart generator knows no null type; no three-valued logic in PATCH. | [R-API-03](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0053](adr/0053-no-json-null-in-api-and-events.md) |
| D-API-03 | Conservative schemas (`oneOf` only with a discriminator, no `anyOf`, no type arrays) and one schema source in `openapi.yaml`. | Compatibility with oapi-codegen and AsyncAPI parsers (own probes). | [R-API-05](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-API-06](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-04 | Problem `type` relative with the full path, plus `code`. | Self-hosted software has no shared host; RFC 9457 allows it. | [R-API-07](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-05 | `If-Match` with `412` instead of `expected_version` with `409`. | HTTP standard (RFC 9110). | [R-API-08](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-06 | `Idempotency-Key` on POST and PATCH with defined exceptions; only final responses are stored. | A retry after step-up, renewal and throttling must work. | [R-API-09](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-API-24](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-07 | Separate endpoints per login method; OIDC with the server as a confidential client and binding to the app. | No unions; intercepted redirects are worthless. | [R-API-12](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-08 | Uploads as a session made of parts. | One client path for both storage drivers, resumption. | [R-API-13](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-09 | Webhooks always with signature `v1a`, HMAC `v1` can be enabled per subscription. | Asymmetric is preferred; common libraries know only HMAC. | [R-API-14](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0016](adr/0016-webhooks-per-standard-webhooks.md) |
| D-API-10 | All repository content is English (D-W-94, owner, 2026-10-11); this includes the descriptions in the YAML files. Before: descriptions in the YAML files English. | They become comments in the generated code. | [R-API-15](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-11 | Health in the public API with action `health.read`. Superseded by D-L2: health lives on the internal listener. | Draft of the team before the ruling. | [R-API-16](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-12 | Run lifecycle via `startRun`, `resumeRun`, `awaitRunInput`, `finishRun` with a target state, and `cancelRun`. | One action per step; no confusion between canceling and canceled. | [R-API-17](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-13 | Generated code is checked in, the gate checks drift. | Reviews see the effect; builds without the generator. | [R-API-18](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-14 | Neutral titles of the contracts without product names. | The name is configuration ([ADR 0044](adr/0044-working-name-and-branding-as-configuration.md)). | [R-API-19](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-15 | Scopes act like grants: without a grant `404`, with a grant but without the scope `403 scope_missing`. | Same behavior as for people. | [R-API-21](spec/06-api.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-16 | One event envelope for gateway, REST catch-up and webhooks; delivery fields never in the log. | One form for all channels. | [ADR 0052](adr/0052-one-event-envelope-for-gateway-rest-and-webhooks.md), [R-SY-17](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-API-17 | New limits as named configuration values. Current state: the names are unified by W1 and D-K-X3. | No fixed numbers in prose. | [06 20](spec/06-api.md#20-configuration-values-of-this-chapter), [PLAN W1](PLAN.md#known-contradictions-that-m1-resolves) |

### 07 Permissions

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-RE-01 | Permission cache per principal with a global version counter and `valid_until`; every REST request reads the current version. No materialized view. | One truth; expiry by time without a write; revocation takes effect across processes. | [R-RE-01](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-RE-02](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-RE-02 | Tool class per agent (`chat`, `scoped`, `host`) with configured and reported tool class; class `host` is triggered only by an owner or by the person in their ready dev environment (SR2). | Check at invocation, because readiness and groups change without a permission transaction. | [R-RE-05](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [07 SR2](spec/07-permissions.md#sr2-tool-class) |
| D-RE-03 | Acting-for binding (SR9): an agent in a run has at most the permissions of the triggering person. | Closes the confused deputy via the server API. | [R-RE-06](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-RE-04 | Read horizon as a hint, not as a block. | A block would make `channel.create` fail unexpectedly. | [R-RE-07](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-RE-05 | The effective operator implicitly has `write`. | No forgotten leftover when the operator changes. | [R-RE-04](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AZ-13](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-RE-06 | Agents and apps receive only `upload` as a capability; only the owner gives grants to them; `manage` revokes only what it could have created itself. | No self-granting; grants to agents are data flows to processors. | [R-RE-03](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-RE-08](spec/07-permissions.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-RE-07 | Owner permissions only in a session, never via a personal API token (SR5). Current state: the exception is the check without a request, in which the owner status comes from the current owner list without a session ([R-AZ-29](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter), D-W-104). | A stolen token without a device must never open the instance. | [R-AZ-14](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter), [07 SR5](spec/07-permissions.md#sr5-administration-only-by-humans-with-confirmation) |
| D-RE-08 | The last active owner can be neither removed nor disabled (SR8). | The instance must not become orphaned. | [07 SR8](spec/07-permissions.md#sr8-last-owner) |

### 08 Identity

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-ID-01 | The server is the OIDC relying party and the authorization service of the apps; PKCE on both legs; mapping only via `(issuer, subject)` or an enrollment code, never via email. | No provider tokens on devices; email is mutable. | [R-ID-01](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-ID-02](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-ID-02 | Two keys per native device. | Same as D-L10. | [R-ID-04](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-ID-03 | Extensions get child sessions with their own refresh family. | Otherwise simultaneous renewal would be detected as theft. | [R-ID-05](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-ID-04 | Admin console following the BFF pattern with a cookie; after login only the own profile until step-up. | No tokens in the browser; owners read almost everything through the console. | [R-ID-06](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-ID-05 | The confirmation code for approving new devices is typed in. | No blind confirmation of someone else's device. | [R-ID-08](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-ID-06 | No recovery codes; recovery via a new enrollment code or a command on the host. | One permanent secret fewer. | [R-ID-09](spec/08-identity.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 09 Agents

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-AG-01 | The effective adapter feature is the intersection of what the owner allows and what the adapter reports. | The owner can switch features off, but cannot force anything the adapter cannot do. | [R-AG-01](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-02 | Threads keep their fixed operator; main threads use the current one. | A frozen operator would mute channels after a change. | [R-AG-02](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DM-21](spec/03-data-model.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-03 | The trigger decision is deterministic: several mentioned agents are rejected visibly; without `trigger` there is an error message only on an explicit mention; another agent during a run is rejected. | At most one active run per thread; the server does not guess. | [R-AG-03](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AG-05](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AZ-20](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-04 | Guest agents run without a session and are never personal. | One session per thread; a personal guest could be triggered in channels that I1 does not check. | [R-AG-04](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AG-22](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AZ-19](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-05 | The server builds the context of a run with a role and a trust mark per message. | Only `authz` knows the permissions of the authors; protection against prompt injection. | [R-AG-06](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0054](adr/0054-origin-and-trust-marking-in-the-agent-context.md) |
| D-AG-06 | The adapter starts the runtime only after `run.start` has been accepted. | At most one execution per invocation. | [R-AG-07](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-07 | `run.start` also applies to `waiting_input -> running`. | An answer to a question is a trigger and needs I2 and I3. | [R-AG-08](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-08 | `trigger_state`: `received` at the server, `queued` at the adapter, new value `cancelled`. | People see whether the agent has the request; a stop is not an error. | [R-AG-09](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-09 | After a stop, open follow-ups are discarded; after an error they are processed later. | Stop means "nothing more". | [R-AG-10](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-10 | One-time rule per message; "Always allow" only for owners. | An approval card has exactly one decision. | [R-AG-11](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-AG-12](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-11 | Built-in control commands with reserved names; no keyword in running text. | Unambiguous, translatable, never sent to the model. | [R-AG-13](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-12 | Control commands run without a run; model commands run like a message and are rejected during a run. | Clear separation of costs and live box. | [09 10.4](spec/09-agents.md#104-execution) |
| D-AG-13 | Autocomplete never calls a model and answers with an empty result after a deadline. | Typing costs nothing and never hangs. | [R-AG-14](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-14 | Auto title with detection of placeholders at creation. | Real titles are never overwritten. | [R-AG-15](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-15 | Voice input: the agent transcribes, otherwise a server port, otherwise a visible rejection. | No silent loss of a voice message. | [R-AG-16](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-16 | Runs that have not started fail after `run.queued_max_age` with `agent_unavailable`. | A job executed surprisingly late is worse than a visible rejection. | [09 run.queued_max_age](spec/09-agents.md#51-state-machine) |
| D-AG-17 | Hermes adapter: progress through public override points. | Same as D-L7. | [R-AG-18](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-18 | Hermes adapter: three access layers (server, adapter with an allow-list, Hermes list only for `personal`). | No restart on every grant. | [R-AG-19](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-19 | The platform key of the Hermes adapter never changes after commissioning. | Hermes builds the session key from the platform name; a change separates every thread from its session. | [09 17.4](spec/09-agents.md#174-session-key) |
| D-AG-20 | Runner: reported mode via a preflight check in the run environment; `apiKeySource` only as a cross-check. | `apiKeySource` alone does not distinguish a subscription from other paths. | [R-AG-20](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-21 | Runner: `--permission-prompts none`. | Unattended turns never hang on a permission prompt. | [R-AG-21](spec/09-agents.md#19-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-AG-22 | All repository content is English (D-W-94, owner, 2026-10-11), including the planning-state READMEs under `plugins/` and `sdk/` and the package READMEs for the registries. Before: planning-state READMEs under `plugins/` and `sdk/` German, package READMEs for the registries English with the first release. | Owner, 2026-10-11: the instruction was English (D-W-94). Earlier reason: planning documents were German. | [plugins/README.md](../plugins/README.md), [sdk/README.md](../sdk/README.md) |

### 10 Backend policy

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-BR-01 | Cost booking: computed from the price table, which takes precedence over the amount from the adapter, otherwise `unpriced`; no currency conversion; in mode `personal` no amounts in the interface (switch `billing.show_personal_estimates`). | Verifiable and the same for all adapters; personal amounts are not an invoice. | [10 12.1](spec/10-backend-policy.md#121-recording), [10 12.2](spec/10-backend-policy.md#122-display) |
| D-BR-02 | Cost reconciliation runs as a separate app outside the server, off by default; the admin key lives only there. | A break-in into the chat server must not reach the admin access; the server never calls models. | [10 12.4](spec/10-backend-policy.md#124-reconciliation-with-billing), [ADR 0055](adr/0055-cost-reconciliation-as-its-own-app-admin-key-never-in-the-server.md) |
| D-BR-03 | After switching, new deliveries carry the new configured mode; adapters with the old reported mode fail visibly; `set_all` to `personal` only with an explicit list. | No hidden queue, no accidental global switch back. | [10 9.2](spec/10-backend-policy.md#92-what-happens-when-switching) |
| D-BR-04 | Guided flow "switch first, then grant" as one composite request in one transaction; the way back to `personal` is not automated. | One confirmed action; revoking permissions automatically would have too many consequences. | [10 11](spec/10-backend-policy.md#11-guided-flow-grant-a-person-access-in-a-personal-space), [R-AZ-17](spec/05-actions.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 11 Extensions

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-EX-01 | Apps never get `trigger` and `manage`; manifests are uploaded, never fetched by URL; unknown fields are errors; the consent shows target hosts and data outflow. | No granting of permissions by integrations, no SSRF, no typos that take effect silently. | [R-EX-01](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-02](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-03](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-04](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-EX-02 | Outgoing webhooks per Standard Webhooks `v1a`, strictly ordered per subscription, from the event log; SSRF protection. Incoming only `native` and `alertmanager`, mentions are not resolved. | Verification with a public key, no silent loss, no notification abuse. | [R-EX-06](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-07](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-08](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-EX-09](spec/11-extensions.md#14-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 12 Files

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-FI-01 | Upload always through a session with an upfront declaration, in parts of fixed size. | Early rejection, resumable uploads. | [R-FI-01](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-02 | Mode `direct` is the default, `presigned` only with S3 and only after proof of the length binding. | Private networks often cannot reach the storage. | [R-FI-02](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-03 | Retrieval only through the server, no presigned GET; short-lived tickets for players. | `authorize` at retrieval time; revocation takes effect immediately. | [R-FI-03](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0048](adr/0048-file-download-only-through-the-server-with-short-lived-tickets.md) |
| D-FI-04 | One read pass for checksum, size, type and scan. | Large files are read only once. | [R-FI-04](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-05 | Scan fails closed; `AlertExceedsMax yes` is mandatory. | Otherwise unchecked content silently counts as clean. | [R-FI-05](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-FI-06](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-06 | Images are re-encoded in pure Go, orientation and ICC profile are kept. Current state: the original is cleaned losslessly per D-L16, only previews are re-encoded. | Location data never leaves the sender. | [R-FI-07](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0047](adr/0047-remove-image-metadata-losslessly.md) |
| D-FI-07 | A file is attached to at most one message. | Deletion without reference counting. | [R-FI-08](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-08 | Agent invocation with attachments only when all attachments are final. | Agents never get unchecked bytes. | [R-FI-09](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-09 | Transcript by the agent by default. | No additional processor without a decision by the owner. | [R-FI-10](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-10 | No deduplication. | No side channel about the existence of a file; safe deletion. | [R-FI-11](spec/12-files.md#16-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-FI-11 | The object storage port knows only Put, Get with range, Head, Delete, Multipart and presigned upload parts; objects are never overwritten. | The default checksums of some SDK functions break several S3-compatible services; the server knows every key from the database. | [12 4.1](spec/12-files.md#41-port), [12 4.4](spec/12-files.md#44-key-schema) |

### 13 Push and notifications

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-PN-01 | Only the server decides about notifications, also for desktop clients. | One place for all rules. | [R-PN-01](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-02 | Notifications of a run are bundled at the end of the run; `input_needed` ignores `mute`, not the quiet hours. | What matters is "finished"; own runs should not hang. | [R-PN-02](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PN-03](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-03 | Automatic following of threads when writing. | Answers to one's own questions arrive. | [R-PN-04](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-04 | Quiet hours make notifications silent instead of muting them; bundling with the first notification immediately and the rest at the end of the window. | Complete notification center without waking anyone. | [R-PN-05](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-PN-06](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-05 | Separate preview token per push registration. | No mutual lockout through the refresh family. | [R-PN-07](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-06 | No FCM `collapse_key`; the app replaces notifications by tag. | FCM keeps only a few collapse keys per device. | [R-PN-08](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-PN-07 | Notification jobs are created in the triggering transaction. Superseded by [ADR 0051](adr/0051-worker-consumers-of-the-event-log-with-a-cursor.md) (D-AR-08): push is produced in the worker consumer `push` from the event log; the job catalogue in 02 follows this. 13 section 12 and [R-PN-09](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) are aligned. | Originally: exactly one notification job per commit without its own cursor. The lead rejected inserting jobs in the write path, because producers should not know their consumers. | [R-PN-09](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0051](adr/0051-worker-consumers-of-the-event-log-with-a-cursor.md), [02 12.3](spec/02-architecture.md#123-job-catalogue), [13 12](spec/13-push-notifications.md#12-execution-in-the-server) |
| D-PN-08 | A central push relay comes later; every instance uses its own keys. | No collection point for metadata. | [R-PN-10](spec/13-push-notifications.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 14 Search

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-SU-01 | One `tsvector` with language stems and `simple` words; language from the author; query as OR over the instance languages plus `simple`. | One index; identifiers and foreign languages stay findable. | [R-SU-01](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SU-02](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SU-03](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SU-02 | Candidate limit before the ranking, one result type per query, no total counts. | Bounded cost; counts would be expensive and a leak risk. | [R-SU-04](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SU-05](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SU-03 | Highlighting with random markers, output as segments, never HTML. | `ts_headline` does not guarantee safe HTML output. | [R-SU-06](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SU-04 | File text extraction in pure Go, Tika optional as a separate container. | No JVM requirement; parser risk outside the server. | [R-SU-07](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SU-05 | Search as a coordinator with read ports of the owning modules. | Tables of other modules only through ports. | [R-SU-09](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SU-06 | Run steps and the live box are not searchable. | Tool output contains secrets and noise. | [R-SU-08](spec/14-search.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 15 Client architecture

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-CL-01 | Additional packages `auth`, `device_key`, `admin_ui`; Pub workspace without Melos. | Shared logic for app, console and native extensions; one tool fewer. | [R-CL-01](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-CL-02](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-CL-02 | Riverpod and drift on encrypted SQLite; the interface reads only from the local database and short-lived stores; outbox as a table. | Reactive queries, transactions, generated migration tests, encryption. | [R-CL-03](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-CL-08](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0029](adr/0029-client-state-local-database-and-offline-queue.md) |
| D-CL-03 | ES256 for all device keys; two keys per device. | Secure Enclave and StrongBox support only P-256. | [R-CL-04](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-CL-05](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-CL-04 | Notification Service Extension, Share Extension and Android push service native without the Flutter engine. | Time and memory limits of the extensions. | [R-CL-06](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0056](adr/0056-native-extensions-without-the-flutter-engine.md) |
| D-CL-05 | Refresh only under a cross-process lock, the new token is stored before the lock is released. Current state: in addition a separate family per extension, the NSE never renews. | Reuse of a refresh token revokes the family. | [R-CL-07](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-CL-06 | Minimum versions Android API 26 and Windows 10 2004. | Same as D-L12. | [R-CL-12](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-CL-07 | Auto-update via Sparkle natively and MSIX with `.appinstaller`. | The Flutter package for updates is outdated. | [R-CL-09](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0037](adr/0037-app-distribution-and-auto-update-channels.md) |
| D-CL-08 | Client metrics via a dedicated server endpoint to the collector; crash reports only with the instance DSN. | No official Dart OTel SDK; one authorized endpoint instead of an open collector. | [R-CL-10](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0057](adr/0057-client-telemetry-through-a-server-endpoint.md) |
| D-CL-09 | Golden tests with alchemist, `patrol` for device E2E, `integration_test` for Windows and a fake server. | System dialogs and notifications are core paths; `patrol` has no Windows support. | [R-CL-11](spec/15-client-architecture.md#21-decisions-of-this-chapter), [15 20](spec/15-client-architecture.md#20-test-strategy) |

### Design system

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-DS-01 | Brand color spruce (green-tinted petrol), gorse (yellow) as the signal color for everything that waits for a person; neutrals with a cool tint; palette named after a highland landscape. Accepted by the owner on the preview images. | Hardly taken in the field of team chats; works in light and dark; guards in the generator keep the ban list measurable. | [design/README.md](design/README.md#why-these-colors) |
| D-DS-02 | In both themes the sidebar is a brand surface with its own roles (`surface.sidebar*`, `text.on-sidebar*`). | Recognition without decoration; separates navigation from content. | [palette.md](design/palette.md#semantic-roles) |
| D-DS-03 | Typeface Atkinson Hyperlegible Next and Mono. | Same as D-L11. | [typography.md](design/typography.md) |
| D-DS-04 | Phosphor icons from a pinned npm package via semantic names, not via a Flutter package. | Filled variant for states; the Flutter package is not maintained. | [icons.md](design/icons.md) |
| D-DS-05 | Token format DTCG with a resolver and three modifiers (theme, density, motion). | Open standard; no copies per theme. | [ADR 0030](adr/0030-design-tokens-as-one-source-with-a-generator.md), [design/README.md](design/README.md#files) |
| D-DS-06 | Generator in the Python standard library, no Style Dictionary. | Gate without network and without a further supply chain. | [ADR 0030](adr/0030-design-tokens-as-one-source-with-a-generator.md), [tools/design-tokens](../tools/design-tokens) |
| D-DS-07 | Contrast pairings and palette guards in one rule file, checked per theme. | AA and parts of the ban list become tests instead of intent. | [contrast.md](design/contrast.md) |
| D-DS-08 | Numbers of the tokens appear only in generated pages; handwritten pages name token names. | No fixed numbers in docs that no test holds. | [tokens.md](design/tokens.md) |
| D-DS-09 | Accent per instance, space colors and tag colors only as palette keys, never as hex. | Only checked combinations pass AA. | [R-PR-08](spec/01-product.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0044](adr/0044-working-name-and-branding-as-configuration.md) |
| D-DS-10 | People round, agents as a rounded square; live box with a spine in the color of the state. | Distinction without color; state at a glance. | [components.md](design/components.md#1-message) |

### 16 Operations

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-OP-01 | Two listeners; the internal one never trusts headers. | Health and internal webhooks need neither a public path nor trust in headers. | [R-OP-01](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-02 | Header identity only with the public listener on loopback, otherwise the provider stays off and `/readyz` reports it. | Enforces the condition from D-B9 instead of assuming it. | [R-OP-02](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-03 | Tailscale as a sidecar with a shared network namespace in kernel networking, tailnet policy only port 443, Funnel forbidden. | Only this way does the server demonstrably listen only on loopback. | [R-OP-03](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [tailscale.md](operations/tailscale.md) |
| D-OP-04 | Unknown configuration keys abort the start. | A typo never silently sets a default. | [R-OP-04](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-05 | Secrets as references (`secret_ref`) and as files per service, never as environment variables. | Child processes and `docker inspect` show no values. | [R-OP-05](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0011](adr/0011-12-factor-configuration-and-secret-provider.md) |
| D-OP-06 | Escrow of the secrets to an owner key is mandatory. | Without the pepper, the signing key and the backup key a restore is worthless. | [16 5.4](spec/16-operations.md#54-escrow) |
| D-OP-07 | pgBackRest for physical backup, `pg_dump` for portability, restic for objects; off-site append-only. | Proven tools instead of a custom build; protection against ransomware. | [R-OP-06](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OP-07](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OP-08](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OP-09](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0058](adr/0058-backup-with-pgbackrest-object-backup-and-restore-test.md) |
| D-OP-08 | Automatic restore test. | A backup is only proven once it has been restored. | [R-OP-10](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-09 | Expand/contract with a way back of exactly one version; `migrate` requires a fresh backup. | A way back without rolling back the database. | [R-OP-12](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OP-13](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-10 | Feature flags with a kind and a cleanup date, never for security boundaries. | No dead flags, no hidden security switches. | [R-OP-14](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0064](adr/0064-feature-flag-registration-with-a-cleanup-gate.md) |
| D-OP-11 | Status page from internal checks. | Works without the optional observability stack. | [R-OP-15](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-12 | Environments `dev`, `staging`, `prod`; default `prod`; production data never in `dev` or `staging`. | Safe default; a copy would be a data protection incident. | [R-OP-16](spec/16-operations.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OP-13 | Grafana admin password via the `$__file{...}` resolution. | Checked locally: the variant with the file suffix did not work with the binary. | [backends.md](observability/backends.md#2-local-grafana-stack) |
| D-OP-14 | Auxiliary services with pinned image versions as Compose variables; Renovate keeps them current. | Repeatable installation. | [configuration.md 9](operations/configuration.md#9-compose-variables), [ADR 0041](adr/0041-dependency-updates-with-renovate.md) |
| D-OP-15 | Operations diagrams next to their documents. | Own directories, no paths that belong to other areas. | [operations/diagrams](operations/diagrams/backup-flow.mmd), [observability/diagrams](observability/diagrams/telemetry-pipeline.mmd) |

### 17 Observability

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-OB-01 | Fan-out in the collector via forward connectors, every backend an overlay. | Several backends without conflicts when merging. | [R-OB-01](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0034](adr/0034-opentelemetry-through-a-collector-local-default-stack.md) |
| D-OB-02 | Data protection twice (at the source and in the collector) with a canary test. | A code error alone does not bring content to the outside. | [R-OB-02](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OB-03 | People only as an HMAC pseudonym in spans and logs, never on metrics; in-app statistics without values per person. | Debugging yes, performance monitoring of individuals no. | [R-OB-03](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OB-04 | Tail sampling in the collector; the SDK records everything; a client cannot force sampling. | Errors are never lost by chance. | [R-OB-04](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-OB-05](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OB-05 | Histograms with fixed boundaries, every SLO threshold is a boundary. | An SLI from buckets is exact only at boundaries. | [R-OB-06](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0035](adr/0035-slos-with-error-budgets-and-burn-rate-alerts.md) |
| D-OB-06 | `correlation_id` is the trace ID of the triggering request. | Connects events, audit and traces without a second ID. | [R-OB-07](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-OB-07 | All repository content is English (D-W-94, owner, 2026-10-11), including the runtime texts in operations artifacts. Before: runtime texts in operations artifacts English. | Instance operators worldwide. | [R-OB-09](spec/17-observability.md#12-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 18 Release

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-RL-01 | Version and changelog per component, tags `<component>/vX.Y.Z`, no `latest`; apps: the same build moves from beta to stable; signature, SBOM and attestation. | Independent releases; what ships is what was tested. | [R-RL-01](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-RL-02](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-RL-03](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-RL-04](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0036](adr/0036-releases-semver-per-component-signed-sbom-provenance.md) |
| D-RL-02 | Apps are white label; each organization builds and signs them itself with `distribution.yaml`. | Push keys belong to the instance; no central push forwarding. | [R-RL-05](spec/18-release.md#13-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0037](adr/0037-app-distribution-and-auto-update-channels.md) |

### 19 Security

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-SE-01 | Signed checkpoints of the audit log outside the database, canonical form according to RFC 8785. | The hash chain alone does not detect a complete rewrite. | [19 SEC-AUD-2](spec/19-security.md#12-supply-chain-backups-and-audit-integrity), [ADR 0021](adr/0021-hash-chained-audit-log-separate-from-telemetry.md) |
| D-SE-02 | Clients do not load third-party resources from content. | No leak through image URLs, no tracking of readers. | [R-SE-05](spec/19-security.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-SE-03 | One egress client with an IP check at connection setup; a lint forbids other HTTP clients. | SSRF protection in one place, also against DNS rebinding. | [R-SE-04](spec/19-security.md#15-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 20 Privacy

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-DP-01 | IP addresses in the audit only as HMAC with destroyable keys. | Investigable for a period, afterwards unlinkable without breaking the chain. | [R-DP-03](spec/20-privacy.md#11-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-DP-02 | Defaults: no default retention period for content, but a required reason in the configuration (`retention.content_none_reason`, otherwise a warning; changed by D-W-22); account deletion pseudonymizes; deletion journal outside the database; no statistics per person; read state never for others. | The controller sets the retention periods; a restore does not bring back deleted data; codetermination-friendly. | [R-DP-01](spec/20-privacy.md#11-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DP-02](spec/20-privacy.md#11-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DP-04](spec/20-privacy.md#11-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DP-06](spec/20-privacy.md#11-decisions-of-this-chapter) |

### 21 Dev isolation

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-DV-01 | Isolation only with a hypervisor boundary; a Linux VM per person as the default; readiness as a record with checks, red team and owner approval; other people's environments only with an organization key in this milestone. | Only the VM holds against injection and misconfiguration; I1 is defined per agent. | [R-DV-01](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DV-02](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DV-03](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DV-05](spec/21-dev-isolation.md#9-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0046](adr/0046-multi-user-dev-environment-only-with-isolation.md) |

### 22 Import and migration

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-IM-01 | The import is a separate process that uses an import interface; Discord only with GET; the server never talks to Discord. | No server-side loading of third-party URLs, no Discord token in the server; a dry run provably writes nothing. | [R-IM-01](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [ADR 0043](adr/0043-discord-import-read-only-dry-run-origin-key.md) |
| D-IM-02 | The dry run is the default and writes nowhere; contradictory options end before any call. | A forgotten switch must not create anything. | [R-IM-02](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-03 | A separate Discord bot account with read permissions only, never the token of a production agent. | Rate limits per bot; minimal permissions. | [R-IM-03](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-04 | Mirror channels are read-only, even for owners. | A channel never has two authoritative locations. | [R-IM-07](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-DM-25](spec/03-data-model.md#8-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-05 | Imported threads carry the planned operator. | Otherwise they would remain without an agent after the switchover. | [R-IM-08](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-06 | Earlier sessions are bound exactly once on the first trigger, fail open. | A failure must never silence the person. | [R-IM-10](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-07 | Import without notifications and without an unread backlog. Current state: through `threads.unread_floor_seq` instead of read states per reader (D-K-I4). | Old content should not nudge anyone. | [R-IM-05](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter), [R-SY-24](spec/04-sync-protocol.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-08 | Reactions and later edits are not carried over. | Cost per emoji and message; the settle rule makes late edits rare. | [R-IM-09](spec/22-import-migration.md#17-decisions-of-this-chapter) |
| D-IM-09 | Rulings of this instance (installation, agents, pilot) and the scope of the private deny list are not in the product. | White label; the product only knows the mechanism. | [ADR 0002](adr/0002-two-repos-public-product-private-ops-repo.md), [ADR 0042](adr/0042-deny-list-scanner-with-the-list-from-the-ops-repo.md) |

### 23 Quality and repository

| ID | Decision | Reason | Location |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-QA-01 | `make check` consists of exactly `gates`, `denylist` and `sonar`; the CI jobs call exactly these targets. | Parity between local and CI by construction. | [R-QA-01](spec/23-quality.md#10-rulings) |
| D-QA-02 | Superseded by D-W-92: every job runs on a fixed GitHub-hosted label; public-only jobs (CodeQL, Scorecard, Pages) with their own condition; a tool enforces both. | The repository is public from the first push; a switch in `runs-on` no longer exists. | [R-QA-03](spec/23-quality.md#10-rulings), [R-QA-04](spec/23-quality.md#10-rulings), [ADR 0039](adr/0039-only-github-hosted-runners-public-from-the-first-push.md) |
| D-QA-03 | Merge only by squash. | Same as D-LS-12. | [push-discipline.md](quality/push-discipline.md#protection-of-main-ruleset) |
| D-QA-04 | Semgrep only with the project's own rules. | Registry rules are not version-bound and have their own license. | [R-QA-05](spec/23-quality.md#10-rulings) |
